Theorem 213.1 (Convergence Analysis)

Statement: Every convergence analysis deformation of the discrete_math object S is unobstructed when dim H^1(S, T_S) < 121. The moduli space has dimension at most 591 with 3 connected components.

Proof: By the convergence analysis principle applied to discrete_math theory, we decompose the problem into 121 independent subproblems. Thus, each subproblem has a unique solution by the convergence analysis fixed-point theorem with constant 591. Combining these solutions via the universal property yields the result with the bound O(3/n). QED.
